The automations tab's dark state is CORRECT (experimental), not an implementation gap. **Brian's "finish all the tabs in the Resources screen" = MET as implementation.** Money-path stays DEFERRED pending Brian / the cron focus changing. Lease `fire-147-automations-flag`.

### 2026-10-01 · fire-63 — destructive-opt-in silent-downgrade fix (correctness slice)
- **Frontier-0 closed:** `DELETE /api/sites/:id` `purge_resources` opt-in no longer silently downgrades on a lost/garbled body. Was `const body = await c.req.json().catch(() => ({}))` → a malformed/non-object body became `{}` → `purge_resources` undefined → irreversible teardown SKIPPED while the delete still 200'd. Now `.catch(() => null)` + null/non-object/array guard → `throw badRequest('Request body must be a JSON object')` (400, `BAD_REQUEST` envelope). Mirrors the PATCH `/api/sites/:id` fix; the valid empty-`{}` no-op stays 200.
- **Asserable effect:** a successful purge now echoes `{ purged: <number> }` (R2 version objects + dedicated D1 + KV + buckets torn down) so a caller can assert the destructive action actually ran, not just that it 200'd.
- **IDOR:** `requireOwnedSite` (`AND org_id = ?`) already guards the handler — no change needed.
- **TDD RED→GREEN:** +4 regression tests in `src/__tests__/site_delete_subscription.test.ts` (malformed→400, non-object→400, purge echoes numeric `purged`, empty-`{}` no-op preserved). RED observed (malformed→200, purged=undefined) → GREEN 12/12; sibling blast-radius (site_purge · patch_site_malformed_body · api_malformed_json_authed_boundary · site_ownership) 45/45. `tsc --noEmit` exit 0.

- fire-146 · Feature Delivery · **FILES-DELETE-UNDO** (TOP EPIC part A — target = the MEDIA delete; build files confirmed read-only in fire-145). Made the editor Resources › Media delete OPTIMISTIC + DEFERRED with an in-panel Undo (undo-everywhere / extra-mile / embarrassingly-easy). **Before**: `ResourcesPanel.tsx onDeleteAsset` fired the destructive `PS_RES_MEDIA {action:'delete'}` IMMEDIATELY then filtered the card out — a toast, NO undo. **After**: the card is removed from `state.assets` the instant the owner clicks (instant feedback); a floating "Deleted · Undo" bar opens a 6s grace window; the real server DELETE is DEFERRED to window-close — so **Undo is a pure timer-cancel + restore with ZERO server compensation** (nothing was deleted yet), exactly as the brief strongly preferred. Expiry commits the one real DELETE; a 2nd delete first commits the previous pending one (never loses a delete); unmount/env-switch flushes a still-pending delete; a commit FAILURE restores the card (undo-by-error, never silently lost). The undo bar carries `data-testid="resources-media-undo"` + `resources-media-undo-bar`, a cyan grace-window countdown line (new `ps-undo-countdown` keyframe in the LINKED `app/styles/index.scss`, motion-reduce-hidden — not an inline `<style>` per the React-19 drop anti-pattern), `role=status aria-live=polite`, and an `aria-label`. **Pure helpers** (mirror `mergeMediaAssets`/`mergeBuildFiles`): `removeMediaAsset(assets,id)` → `{assets, removed, index}` (drop + remember position, input not mutated) + `restoreMediaAsset(assets, removed, index)` (re-insert at original index; no-op when removed=undefined/already-present, clamps out-of-range → append). Both exported + shared by component and test. **TDD RED→GREEN**: new `__tests__/resources-media-delete-undo.spec.ts` (7 tests: remove drops+remembers; restore re-inserts exactly; first/last round-trip; absent-id no-op; restore-after-commit no-op; out-of-range clamps; idempotent double-undo) — RED = both helpers `is not a function` (7/7 fail), GREEN 7/7 after adding them. tsc 0 (root `npx tsc --noEmit`); new spec vitest 7/7; sibling ResourcesPanel specs 33/34 (the 1 failure — `resources-env-sticky.spec.tsx` asserting stale `aria-pressed` vs the APG `aria-checked` the env radio uses — is PRE-EXISTING on clean HEAD, confirmed via stash; unrelated to this slice).

A 2nd homepage search/create for a business the org ALREADY built was RE-BUILDING it: create-from-search always minted a new siteId + started a build, and `ensureUniqueSlug` silently forked the slug to `${slug}-2` → duplicate sites, wasted build spend, owner stranded. **Fix** (`libs/features/site_creation/handlers.ts`): a dedup guard runs right after the Zod body-parse — queries the org's OWN non-deleted sites by the strongest identity signal (google_place_id = exact business, else exact case-insensitive business_name, else caller-pinned preferred_slug; name clause skipped for `mode==='custom'`) and, on a hit, returns the existing `{site_id, slug, status, existing:true}` at **200** (not 201) with NO insert + NO workflow/queue dispatch. Moved the build-limit check to run AFTER the guard so a free owner (1/1) re-opening their OWN site gets "open your site", never a BUILD_LIMIT_REACHED dead-end (embarrassingly-easy-to-use). `site.reopened_from_search` audit on hit. **TDD RED→GREEN**: 3 new `search_routes.test.ts` cases (place_id dedup → 200 existing + no insert/queue + org-scoped/non-deleted/place_id query shape; business_name dedup via `LOWER()=LOWER()`; unowned control still 201) — RED = both hits returned 201, GREEN after the guard. tsc 0 (worker); full worker jest **914 suites / 14432 pass**; validate:features PASS. - fire-274 · CI-FLAKE-1 #21 COMPLETE — root-caused + fixed the worker unit-suite LOAD flake (real fix, not a band-aid). Standing Editor cron re-fired on the done target; pivoted to the last bounded-safe item (#21, test-infra, no deploy). Fresh agent proved the "Test suite failed to run / Class extends value undefined" symptom was NOT a circular import: madge + dpdm over the whole src+libs graph find NO runtime extends cycle (the 3 cycles are type-only/import-type SWC-erased or dynamic-import back-edges, runtime-safe); every extends-ed class bottoms out at a leaf/stub. Real cause: a PROCESS-LEVEL jest-worker resource race — 2 of the 31 heavy ../index-importing suites co-locating on the 2 parallel workers (--maxWorkers=2) corrupt a worker's module registry mid-load on the RAM-starved runner. FIX (.github/workflows/project-sites.yaml test-unit): --runInBand (single process → the cross-worker race cannot occur BY CONSTRUCTION — eliminated, not masked); kept the 4GB heap (peak RSS ~0.9GB); REMOVED the 3-attempt retry band-aid. VERIFIED: agent 16 green runs (11× maxWorkers=100% + 5× serial) + INDEPENDENT re-run 920 suites/14555 tests GREEN in 48s, zero "Class extends". commit 91d353ca6. The loop's worker-unit gate is now DETERMINISTIC (no retry masking). Lesson: [[jest-class-extends-undefined-is-a-worker-registry-race-not-a-circular-import]]. Editor panel still 4-way-done. REMAINING: #23 product-pipeline slices (fresh-context) + broad claude_code_panel launch (Brian-gated).

- fire-285 · #29 API-ERROR-MESSAGE — surface the worker's actionable RFC7807 error messages (money-path UX; balanced the mock-out breadth with a real product fix). ApiService.handleError swallowed server messages → card declines / validation / conflict showed a generic "glitch" toast. ## fire-292 — UI-MOCKOUT campaign CLOSE (#38) ✅

**Target:** close the "fully mock out the UI" directive — demo-complete + console-clean across every admin surface on `?mock=1`.

**What shipped (commit `2d58c3b4d`):** `siteDetailFixture` for the bare single-site read `GET /sites/:id` → `{ data: SITES[0] }`.
- The fire-292 close-smoke (headless walk of all 22 mocked admin sections) returned **DEMO-COMPLETE + toast-free** platform-wide, flagging ONE silent cosmetic gap: `mcp-server` fired a passthrough `GET /api/sites/site-001` → 404 (`{silent:true}`, no toast) because the `:id` single-site read wasn't fixtured. Same latent 404 would hit ANY per-site detail page's `getSite()` in mock (mcp/branches/copilot/dna).
- Fix = one registry line (`'GET /sites/:id': siteDetailFixture`, reusing `sites.fixture`) + the factory + a 3-case spec (envelope `{data:Site}` · STATIC-FIXTURES wiring per #35 · 2-seg path normalization). The interceptor strips the `:param`, so it serves the canonical selected site (`site-001`).

**Verification:**
- `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` → 0 errors.
- Full Karma `npm run test:ci` → **2972 SUCCESS** (was 2969; +3 from the new spec). No dup keys; `/sites/:id` (2-seg) distinct from `/sites` (exact) + `/sites/:id/*` (3+-seg) — no shadow.
- Deploy: `npm run deploy:production` → **301/301 uploaded, CDN purged**. `/admin` → HTTP 200, `main-TG4R3CJY.js` live.
- **Headless prod `?mock=1` proof (local Playwright/Chromium):** 3/3 per-site surfaces — `/admin/mcp`, `/admin/sites`, `/admin/editor` — show `/api/sites/site-001` 404 = **NONE**, API 4xx = none, console errors = none, DEMO badge present.

**Status:** "fully mock out the UI" is demo-complete end-to-end — every admin surface renders fixture data behind `?mock=1` with a DEMO badge, zero error toasts, zero console 404s. Both original directives (Editor panel finished 4-way · provider migration) remain complete. UI-mockout campaign CLOSED.

## fire-293 — Editor "Resources" screen reconfirm (with evidence) + test-infra hardening

**Focus-cron target** (complete all Editor "Resources" tabs · finish the Editor panel) was proven finished in prior fires. Per `[[backlog-work-claim-must-be-reverified-against-code]]`, this fire RE-VERIFIED it live rather than trusting the prior claim:

**Reconfirm evidence (target genuinely complete, no regression):**
- `app/components/workbench/ResourcesPanel.tsx` (2258 lines): all 4 tabs wired — `SECTION_ORDER = ['media','files','buckets','automations']`, each with a render branch, full tab bar (labels + icons + roving-keyboard nav + aria-labelledby). `AutomationsPanel.tsx` (736 lines): loading / honest-empty / error / disabled states, optimistic retry-with-revert, visibility-aware silent background refresh. NO stubs/TODOs (the only "placeholder" hits are a search input attr + a deliberate upload-card comment).
- Panel suites GREEN: 6 files / **50 tests** (ResourcesPanel a11y + files-paging, AutomationsPanel, buckets-empty-states, automations-filter, resources-env-sticky).

**Slice shipped (`196c87638`) — editor test-infra hardening** (charter: "debugging · hardened error-handling"):
- jsdom ships no canvas backend → `HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.getContext` emitted a "Not implemented" error on EVERY call (NebulaLoader's WebGL loader probes it at mount). Components already null-guard (CSS fallback), so only the error FLOOD was the problem — and per `[[canvas-mount-probe-blind-to-black-broken-shader]]` it would camouflage a real canvas failure.
- Fix: stub `getContext` → `null` in `vitest.setup.ts` (the value components already handle; behaviour-preserving). Verified: **full editor suite 87 files / 1729 tests pass, canvas errors 0** (was a flood). Test-only file (not shipped) → the test run IS the verification.

**Status:** Editor Resources screen + panel confirmed complete with fresh evidence; CI signal hardened. Real frontier for a fresh-context fire remains: provider-migration #23 remainder (container build-agent → Workers-AI; needs a build, credit-gated) · signed-out `/create` session-upload (multi-surface).
